Never use a bare `<header>` inside a card or dialog: `src/styles.css` paints it as the fixed 58px app bar; the address card and dialog heads are divs. `.soListCard` must stay `overflow:visible` or the register card clips the filter panel.
- The Create / Edit Sales Order page opens with the full-bleed `div.soCreateHead` (one 40px `button.soBack` beside `div.soHeadText`), flush under the Organisation / Branch bar through `.app main:has(.soCreatePage){max-width:none!important;padding:0!important}` plus `.soCreatePage>:not(.soCreateHead)` for the gutter - the same recipe the Create Item and Create Customer pages use. Never restore the inline `button.ivLink` back link. When a customer is selected, `SalesDocumentFields` shows `div.soCustomerRef` (billing and shipping address cards, each with a pencil, plus the customer's GST treatment and GSTIN) instead of two free-text address inputs, and the pencil opens `div.soAddressLayer` / `section.soAddressDialog`, which edits `form.billing` / `form.shipping` for **this order only**. The panel is two wide address columns over one full-width tax strip (GST treatment / GSTIN / PAN); keep that shape rather than returning to three narrow columns, which left a one-line address in a mostly empty box, and keep the address flattened for display by `inlineAddress` with no `white-space:pre-line`, because one part per line made the cards as tall as the address. The create-state head carries the title alone - the hint renders only while editing. The form header is four columns (number, date, customer, reference on one row) with `.soPlaceField` spanning two so the second row fills exactly; do not put the header back to three columns. The create page must keep `html:has(.soCreatePage)` in the scroll rule alongside `body` and `#root` - copying only the body selector leaves `html` at `height:100%;overflow:hidden` and the page cannot scroll at all, even though it reports a taller scrollHeight. The order's billing and shipping addresses collapse to one card with a "Shipping uses the same address." note while they match, and only split into two when they differ; keep the "Use a different shipping address" action, or a matching pair can never be made different, and keep the shared-card editor writing both billing and shipping. Remember that src/invoice-workspace.css is imported after src/sales-orders.css and styles bare elements inside .invoiceWorkspace (h2 at 23px, h3, small, textarea), so any component rule on this page must be prefixed with .soCreatePage to outrank it - the head title silently rendered at 23px until it was. The order items table must stay on `table-layout:fixed` with its percentage columns: it is the only thing that guarantees no horizontal scroll, because a real amount is far wider than the em dash that stands in for it while the table is empty. Keep `.ivItemSearch{width:185px}` and `.ivTaxSelect{width:auto}` overriding the 230px and 215px the shared sheet gives them, or the item and tax cells overflow again. The order items Tax cell fills its column as a grid of the rate selector and the price-tax treatment side by side (`.soCreatePage .ivLineTax`), overriding the stacked `.ivLineTax` the invoice page still uses; keep the summary truncating so the table stays at its 1150px floor. The shell padding must keep matching the Create Item page: `body:has(.soCreatePage) .app>main{…scrollbar-gutter:auto}` or the head bar stops 15px short of the right edge, and `.soCreatePage .salesOrderForm{width:auto;margin:12px 0 0}` - `width:100%` plus the gutter margins overflows the content edge by 24px, and the 12px top margin is the gap under the head bar that the other create pages leave. That panel is scoped to `kind==='order'`: the invoice page must keep its plain address inputs, and the editor must never write back to the customer master.
- The Sales Orders register is eight columns - Order (SO number over order date), Customer, Branch & Organisation, Amount, Status, Payment status, Created by, Actions - with no Reference or Expected shipment column, no blue "Sales" eyebrow and no record count in the bar. Payment status is derived from the linked invoice through the engine`s `paymentStatus(state, invoice)` and reads "Not invoiced" before an invoice exists; never hardcode it. The toolbar holds one `details.soFiltersMore` disclosure with Payment status, Customer, Organisation, Branch, Created by, Order date and Expected shipment ranges. Save stamps `organizationId` / `organizationName` / `branchId` / `branchName` from `getCurrentOrganizationContext()` and `createdBy` from the acting role, always as `form.x||stamped` so a re-save never overwrites a stored value. Do not add Fulfilment or Payment filters: an order stores no fulfilment state and no payment mode.
- The Create / Edit Sales Order page is one section per job - Customer, Order details, Order items, Billing summary, then the collapsed Additional details - and the customer's identity is a read-only definition list. `dl.soFactList` holds GST treatment, GSTIN and PAN on three narrow tracks with the two addresses on wider ones; the labels are `dt` (12px muted) and the values `dd` (13px strong), the same pattern `itemDetailFacts` uses on the Item Details page, so nothing in it reads as an input. `chooseCustomer` copies every value from the customer master. The order's payment terms come from the shared `PAYMENT_TERM_DAYS` table plus a Custom row, and the day count comes from `customerTermDays()` - never `c.days||'30'`, which turned a Due-on-Receipt customer into Net 30. `convertSalesOrder` carries the order's organisation/branch and the customer's receivable ledger onto the invoice (period locking resolves from those, and `command('post')` posts to `i.receivableAccount||s.config.ar`), and it refuses a legacy order whose `taxRate`/`adjustment` is non-zero rather than dropping the withholding. The register menu carries Duplicate, Export and the destructive Delete (disabled with its reason when an invoice exists), exports through the pure `ordersCsv`, and every write appends to an additive optional `auditTrail` shown as History in Additional details. The panel shows the billing address only, with the labelled Edit as its single control: when the customer's shipping address matches, the row prints `Shipping address is the same.` plus the split action rather than a second address, and a separate Shipping address row appears only when the two differ. The Billing summary block is `width:min(420px,100%);margin-left:auto` so its heading sits directly above the totals panel, and its `Item tax and price treatment are filled from the Item master.` note is rendered for the invoice page only. Place of supply must stay OUT of this panel - it is an editable field and belongs in the Order details section, or the row mixes a select in among read-only values and looks broken. The page opens on `div.soOrderTop` - the Customer card left at 40%, Order details right at 60%, `align-items:stretch` so both cards are the same height (drop the stacked-card margin inside the row with `.soOrderTop>.ivCard+.ivCard.ivCard{margin-top:0}`, or the 14px margin - which a grid item includes in the box it stretches - leaves the right card 14px shorter), stacking to one column below 1080px. Inside the 40% column the read-only facts go one per line (label left, value right, a hairline between rows) instead of five across, and `.soFactItemWide` must be released to `grid-column:auto` there or its `span 2` creates an implicit second column. The customer itself is chosen with the shared searchable combobox (`src/SearchSelect.jsx`) inside `div.soCustomerRow` (capped at 520px) as an ordinary field row, never a native select; Organisation and Branch live in Order details, which is one `soFieldRow4` four-column grid over eight fields and therefore renders as two complete rows of four (Organisation, Branch, Sales order number, Sales order date / Reference number, Place of supply, Payment terms, Due date). Keep the `soFactCaption` above it, keep `align-items:start` so a short value does not leave a tall empty box, and keep `dt{display:flex;align-items:center;min-height:30px}` so an address label - whose row also carries the Edit button - does not drop below the plain values beside it. Order details is one `soFieldRow` three-column grid over six fields, which renders as two rows of three (number, date, reference / place of supply, payment terms, due date); there is no five- or six-column row. The action bar is `ivFooter.soActionBar` and is genuinely sticky - it only looked broken because `#root` carried `overflow-y:auto` in the page's scroll rule, and any overflow other than visible makes that element the nearest scrollport; `html` now keeps the overflow and `body` / `#root` are `overflow:visible`. Attachments use `label.soUploadDrop` + `ul.soFileList` (drag and drop feeds the same `readFiles` the input uses, the limit printed is the 5 MB the code enforces) - never restore the bare `<label>Attachments<input type="file">`. All of it is `kind==='order'` only; the invoice page keeps its three-column grid, plain address inputs, editable unit and its own totals.
- The **invoice detail screen** is one card under an arrow-led full-bleed head, not a breadcrumb: `div.ivDetailHead` (40px `button.ivDetailBack` + `Invoice details` + a hint naming the document) sits flush under the working-context strip through `body:has(.ivDetailOpen) .app>main{...max-width:none;padding:0}` and the marker `const detailOpen=page==='Invoices'&&!!invoice`. `section.ivDetailSheet` then holds, in order, `div.ivDetailIdentityRow` (number, lifecycle pill, customer, actions), `div.ivDetailStats` (the four figures), `div.ivDetailTabs` and `div.ivDetailTabBody`; the card is `overflow:visible` because the More actions menu opens inside it. The tab row is the module's left-aligned underline row (`display:flex;gap:0;height:42px;padding:0 12px`, `flex:0 0 auto` buttons on a 12px gutter) - never three equal columns spread across the card. One rule governs every section title inside the card (`.ivDetailSheet .ivSectionTitle`), and the card padding is tight: identity `14px 18px 12px`, figures `11px 18px`, overview `14px` with a `12px` gap, panels and cards `14px 16px`, fact list `12px 18px`. **Items and Payments are sections of Overview, never tabs** - the tabs are exactly Overview / Accounting / Activity - and the blue `SALES INVOICE` eyebrow (`span.ivEyebrow`), the breadcrumb (`div.ivDetailTop`) and their CSS are deleted. The action row keeps the lifecycle buttons and the primary Receive payment; Preview & print, Send or share invoice, Accounting details, View journal, View ledger, Duplicate (`duplicateInvoice`, which drops id/number/revision/journalId/arAccount/postedAt/posted/status/sourceOrder/attachments) and the destructive Cancel invoice live in the More actions menu. `InvoiceOverview` is the invoice itself in five blocks - Customer, Invoice summary (Subtotal / Discount / GST / Round off, then Total / Paid / Balance), Items (Item / Qty / Rate / Tax rate / Amount), Payments (count badge and one row per payment) and a collapsed More details (`details.ivOverviewMore`) holding Order number and status, Invoice date, Due date, Place of supply, Salesperson, Organisation, Branch, Notes and Terms & conditions. Do not reintroduce the standalone Organisation & branch and Invoice information panels, the Amount summary / Payment summary / Accounting summary / Related documents cards, or the five-across fact grid: the reference data belongs in More details, the money in the summary block and the accounting on its own tab. The Overview is the Item Details language: `div.ivOverviewGrid` holds the Customer and More details sections side by side at half width each (stacking below 900px) as `itemDetailCard` + `itemDetailSectionTitle` + a two-column `itemDetailFacts` grid - borrow those classes rather than restyling, because that is the border, title strip and dt/dd tokens the user asked to match - with the two addresses spanning the grid, and More details is a section card, not a disclosure. The tab row is the Item Details row shared by class (`nav.itemDetailTabs.ivDetailTabs`) and restated verbatim under `.ivDetailSheet` so the host sheet's bare `button` rule (9px 13px padding, 6px radius, min-height 36px) cannot restyle it whichever sheet loads last - tab buttons `0 12px`, square corners, 13px/600, centred, `#f5f8fd` hover, 2px `#3478f6` active underline, the count pill and the 520px equal-cell fallback. Only `overflow:auto` (four tabs must not clip) and `min-height:0` depart from `src/items.css`, and a test diffs the two rule sets property by property. The tabs are Overview / Payments / Accounting / Activity - Payments is its own tab between Overview and Accounting and renders `invoicePaymentRows(invoice,db)` - and the figures row leads with **Order number and status**, not a second copy of the invoice status the header already shows (and that fact is no longer repeated in More details). `.ivDetailStats>span` needs `align-items:flex-start`, or a pill stretches to the full tile width. The Overview is Customer / Items / More details with no descriptive subtitles. The summary lives INSIDE the Items panel, right-aligned under the table (`section.ivInvoiceSummary{width:min(420px,100%);margin-left:auto}`), the Customer panel states every fact the master holds (contact, email, phone, GSTIN, PAN, GST treatment, place of supply) with Billing and Shipping addresses as full-width `.ivFactWide` rows, and every status on the page renders the shared `StatusPill` through `INVOICE_TONES` / `PAYMENT_TONES` - never a page-local badge. Organisation and branch resolve through `getScopeOrganisations()` (id or code) and are never invented - and the invoice save stamps them from `getCurrentOrganizationContext()` keeping any stored value, because a directly created invoice used to record no scope at all.
- On the same day the Create / Edit Sales Order order-items line was finished and the order was given a required organisation and branch. The **Unit** column is read-only text taken from the Item master (`span.soUnitValue`, an em dash until an item is chosen) - never restore an input there, but keep it on the `kind==='order'` branch: the Invoice create page shares this component and must keep its editable unit input, because src/invoice-engine.js refuses a line with no unit and a free-typed invoice line has nothing to copy one from. The scope message never fires on the invoice page (it passes no organisations, so both reference sets are empty). The **Discount** number yields (`flex:1 1 0`, `width:auto!important`, `min-width:0`) so the `%` / `₹` selector can keep `flex:0 1 54px;min-width:46px` inside an `overflow:hidden` row, because the shared sheet's `width:100%` on every line control plus `select{width:55px!important}` could not both fit and the selector was pushed out of the cell and clipped. The remove-line action is a 32px square at `#b42318` on `#fff5f4` with a 7px radius and a centred 16px `IconTrash`. `SalesDocumentFields` now takes `organisations` and renders required **Organisation \*** / **Branch \*** selects for `kind==='order'` only, seeded from `getCurrentOrganizationContext()` with the branch cleared when the organisation changes; `SalesOrders` reads `getAccessibleOrganizations()` once and both are required before `saveSalesOrder` runs. `itemScopeError(item,scope)` / `orderScopeError(lines,items,scope)` in `src/sales-order-service.js` are the only scope rule: the item's `organizationIds` (falling back to `organizationId`) is compared with the chosen organisation and, for a `Goods` that `trackInventory`, its `branchIds` (falling back to `warehouseId`) with the chosen branch - a Service, an item that tracks no inventory, and an item that records no organisation scope at all are never blocked. Compare **reference sets**, not single values: the Item create form seeds an organisation id while its scope picker writes the organisation code, and the opening-stock branch field accepts an id or a name, so a single-value comparison reports valid items as out of scope. The sales line keys its item as `line.item` (Inventory Adjustments keys it `itemId`), and the failure is reported both on the line through `small.itemError` and through `errors.save`. Finally, `.soCreatePage table.ivLineTable` - with the element - is required rather than `.soCreatePage .ivLineTable`: `.invoiceWorkspace .ivLineTable{min-width:980px}` ties on class-level specificity and is imported later, so the class-only rule never applied its `min-width:0` and the table kept a 980px floor, measuring 0px overflow at 1440 but 67px at 1280, 247px at 1100 and 362px at 980. Keep the element in that selector, and keep `.soCreatePage .ivLineTable .ivSearchInput input{width:100%!important;max-width:100%}` ahead of the shared 230px `!important`.
- The Inventory Adjustments row menu always offers View, Edit, Duplicate, Export and Delete plus the one status action, and Edit and Delete are shown disabled with the engine's reason rather than hidden: only a Draft is editable, and a record that posted its journal is never deleted because the ledger would lose an entry with nothing pointing at it. Keep `actionEntries` as the single builder for the register menu and the detail menu, keep `Delete` on the `cancel` duty, and keep the engine's `delete` command refusing a posted record and writing an `inventory-adjustment-delete` audit event.
- The Inventory Adjustments detail screen opens `src/AdjustmentPreview.jsx` from its Preview button, replacing the detail screen the way Journal Entries opens JournalPreview. It reuses the internal-document preview shell (`jp-*` over `.dpPaper`), never the customer-facing DocumentPreview, and it must never recalculate a line - every figure comes from `storedLines(row)`.
- The Inventory Adjustments register is deliberately six columns: `['Reference','Type / Mode of adjustment','Organisation','Items','Status','Actions']`, with each of the first four carrying two facts (number over date, type over `entryModeLabel`, organisation over branch, item count over the signed amount). Do not split those pairs back into nine columns, and do not re-add Reason, Adjustment account or Created by to the register - they live on the detail screen under Adjustment information, which also states the Entry mode. Keep the signed amount's `iaUp` / `iaDown` colouring inside the Items cell.
- Every status display uses the one shared pill, `src/StatusPill.jsx` over `src/status-pill.css`, with a `tone` of `ok` (default), `neutral`, `warn`, `info`, `violet` or `danger`. The Customers register and detail, the Period Lock register (`CLOSING_TONES`), Inventory Adjustments (`STATUS_TONES`, which also feeds the create/detail head badges) and the new Items Status column all use it. Do not reintroduce `.customerStatus`, `.pc-badge.pc-status.*`, `.iaBadge` or `.itemDisabledBadge`, and do not restyle the Chart of Accounts `.am-status` or Journal Entries `.je-status` pills - they are the reference and the shared sheet must keep matching them.
- A contact person''s primary marker is `button.customerPrimaryToggle`, never a checkbox: it reads `Set as Primary Contact` at rest and `Primary Contact` with a check when active, announces `aria-pressed`, and routes through `setPrimaryContact` so exactly one contact is primary. Do not restore the checkbox or the `.customerContactTools .itemCheck` rule.
- Extraordinary care with the tax fields: each `GST_TREATMENTS` entry carries `gstin`, `pan`, `business`, `placeOfSupply`, `countryRequired` and `note`, and those flags - not the page - decide what renders. Only Tax Exempt asks for an exemption reason (`exemptionRequired`), and an Overseas customer hides the Indian state picker, requires the billing country and stores that country as `state`, because the invoice and sales-order screens read `customer.state` as the place of supply.
- `src/gst-lookup.js` is the only thing the browser uses to fetch taxpayer details and it calls exactly one address, the app's own `/api/gst/lookup`. Never add a GST/GSP URL, an API key, a client secret, an access token or a `Bearer` header to anything under `src/`. The credential lives in the Sites worker (`worker/index.js`) as the `GST_API_URL` and `GST_API_KEY` environment secrets and is sent upstream only.
- `worker/index.js` is copied verbatim to `dist/server/index.js` by `scripts/prepare-sites-build.mjs`, so it must stay self-contained: never import from `src/` or any other directory in it. It matches `/api/gst/lookup` by exact pathname before the asset handler, and every other path keeps the existing SPA-fallback contract.
- A failed or unconfigured lookup must never block customer creation: show the standard message, fill the state and PAN the GSTIN itself carries, and leave every taxpayer field editable. Keep the `gstLookup` timestamp/status stamp on the record.
- Do not re-key the customer code. It is prefilled from `nextCustomerCode(rows)` on create and stays required and unique.
- Do not drop `billing`, `shipping` or `state`. They are still the plain strings `src/InvoiceWorkspace.jsx` and `src/SalesOrders.jsx` read. The form additionally stores the structured `billingAddress` / `shippingAddress` objects so editing reopens what was entered, and keeps both shapes in step through `composeAddress` alone. A legacy record that only has the composed string is preserved verbatim by `parseAddress`.
- `contact` is now derived rather than typed: it is the primary contact person's display name, or the customer name for an Individual with no contact persons. It is still written, because register search and the Customer Details page read it, and a legacy free-text `contact` is converted into the first contact row by `contactFromName`.
- Do not make legal name or trade name required, and do not claim `Get Taxpayer Details` fetches them. The decoder reads only what a GSTIN carries (state code, state name, PAN) and there is no GST portal connection; the two names stay optional operator fields that appear only after a verified business GSTIN.
- Do not add a Customer Portal field or setting.
- Legacy rows are normalised by `normaliseCustomer` before editing, so a customer stored before this change (no `billingAddress`, no `gstTreatment`, no `contacts`, `contact` as free text) reopens without losing data and without inventing values.


### The create-page row split is 60/40

The shared sales create row now puts the Customer card at 60% and the Order / Invoice details card at 40%: `.soCreatePage .soOrderTop{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,60fr) minmax(0,40fr);align-items:stretch;gap:14px}`. Measured at 1440x950: Customer 656px, details 437px, both 435px tall, so the equal-height contract survives the swap.

The 40% column cannot hold the four-across field grid, so above the fold `.soOrderTop .soFieldRow4` is two columns and the eight fields read two per row. The fold moved from 1080px to **1240px**, where the cards stack and the grid returns to four across: at 1180px the 40% column came to 333px, which left each field track at 137.75px and truncated every organisation select and every placeholder. A narrower column moves the width at which a layout stops working, so the breakpoint has to move with it. Measured at 1180px afterwards: two 847px cards with 189.3px tracks and nothing truncated. The number field placeholder also shortened from `Automatic if left blank` to `Automatic`, because a 22-character placeholder truncates in a narrow field even where the card is usable.

Validation: `tests/sales-order-ui.test.mjs` and `tests/invoice-detail-ui.test.mjs` are 57/57, with four fold assertions repinned from the 1080px breakpoint to the 1240px one and two placeholder assertions rewritten; the full sweep is 644 tests with 633 passing and the same 11 pre-existing failures; the production build, `prepare-sites-build` and the documentation validator all passed; browser-verified at 1440x950 and 1180x950 on both create pages.

### Edit-customer round trip, create-page captions and the customer empty state

- The detour from either create page to the customer master is: the page stashes its draft under `wayvida-draft-order` / `wayvida-draft-invoice`, sets `wayvida-edit-customer` and `wayvida-return-to`, and navigates; `Customers` opens that record through its own sessionStorage handoff and navigates back on save; the returning page restores the draft in its `useState` initializer and refreshes it through `applyCustomerSnapshot`.
- It was broken in two places, both fixed. (1) A stored record that names no `gstTreatment` of its own was handed the registered default, which then demanded a GSTIN and a PAN it had never held, so the customer form could not be saved and the return line never ran. The default now follows the record's own tax identity: a GSTIN present keeps `Registered Business – Regular`, none opens `Unregistered Business` (read from the one `GST_TREATMENTS` table), and an individual stays `Consumer`. A brand-new customer still opens on the registered default, and the GSTIN/PAN requirement for a declared registered treatment is unchanged. (2) `InvoiceWorkspace`'s draft initializer read the `customers` const declared below it, so a temporal-dead-zone ReferenceError was swallowed by the initializer's own `try/catch` and the draft - already consumed from sessionStorage - was dropped. It now reads the customer list from storage inline, as `SalesOrders` always did; do not tidy that back to the later binding.
- Both create cards are named by their headings alone: the customer caption and the `The organisation and branch ...` caption are deleted. Do not restore them.
- The customer card renders the shared animated empty state (the `customer` variant in `src/EmptyState.jsx`) until `selectedCustomer` resolves, and the eight-fact read-out only after it does; the placeholder values must stay unreachable in the empty branch. `.soCreatePage .soCustomerEmpty` bounds the block so the two cards stay level. The card pencil is gated on the same condition, because choosing a customer overwrites both addresses from the master.
- Validation: the four affected suites are 104/104 with three new cases; the full sweep is 647 tests with 636 passing and the same 11 pre-existing failures; the build, `prepare-sites-build` and the documentation validator passed; both round trips were driven end to end in headless Chrome.

### Pinned action bar, the Billing summary rule and the address dialog gutter

- `.soCreatePage .soActionBar` is `position:sticky;bottom:0;z-index:6` again with its opaque `background:#f6f8fb` and top hairline, and `.soCreatePage .soBarActions` carries `margin-left:auto`. Both halves matter: the position is why the three decisions stay on screen, and the auto margin is why they stay on the **right** - the running totals `<dl>` renders only while the document can be calculated, and a lone flex child under `justify-content:space-between` sits at the left, which is where the buttons were parked. The bar was `position:static` between two passes because a pinned bar covered the Billing summary; it is sticky again on the user's instruction, under the row menus (80) and the address dialog (1200).
- `.soCreatePage .soBillingSummary` no longer carries the `1px solid #eef1f6` top border or the `padding-top:16px` that stood in for it - that was the line reported above the Billing summary. It keeps `width:min(420px,100%)` and `margin:18px 0 0 auto`.
- The address dialog's fields are `div.soAddressFields`, so the old `.soAddressDialog>label` rule was dead; it is replaced by `.soAddressDialog>.soAddressFields{padding:16px 18px 0}`, the same 18px gutter the heading, the note and the footer use.
- These create pages scroll on `documentElement`, not on `.app>main`: driving a scroll in the browser must move `documentElement` (or use a real wheel event), or the measurement silently reads as "nothing moved".
- Validation: `sales-order-ui` + `invoice-detail-ui` 62/62 with two new cases and the action-bar pins rewritten; the full sweep is 649 tests with 638 passing and the same 11 pre-existing failures; the build, `prepare-sites-build` and the documentation validator passed.

### Sales invoices register row menu

- `src/InvoiceRowActions.jsx` renders the row menu in the invoices register, beside the `View` link: the sales order register's `.soMoreButton` / `.soActionMenu` / `.soActionDanger` pattern from `src/sales-order-actions.css`, portalled because the register scrolls inside `.ivScroll`. Entries: Approve and post, Record payment, Make Recurring, Create Credit Note, Duplicate, Cancel Invoice - all rendered, each disabled with the reason sentence when it does not apply.
- The handlers live in `src/InvoiceWorkspace.jsx`: `postInvoice` (`run('post')`), `startPayment` (existing receipt handoff), `openCreditNote` (`wayvida-credit-customer` + `wayvida-credit-invoice` + `QUICK_CREATE_KEY='credit-note'`, then navigate), `duplicateInvoice` (existing), `setCancel` (the page's own cancellation dialog) and `openRecurring`.
- `src/CreditNotes.jsx` consumes `wayvida-credit-invoice` once in the quick-create effect and calls `selectInvoice`, which is now a functional `setForm` update so it can be queued behind `start()` without reading a stale closure.
- `src/invoice-engine.js` owns `RECURRING_FREQUENCIES` and a `recurring` command action: it requires a posted, non-cancelled invoice, validates the cadence and dates, stores `recurring:{frequency,nextDate,endDate,setAt}` on the invoice, clears with `off:true`, and writes the ordinary audit line. It posts nothing and generates nothing - the dialog and the handbook both say the prototype keeps the schedule only.
- Validation: `invoice-engine` 12/12, `invoice-row-actions` 5/5, full sweep 655 tests with 644 passing and the same 11 pre-existing failures; build, `prepare-sites-build` and the documentation validator passed; every entry browser-verified, including the recurring turn-off and a real cancellation.

### Sales invoices register: seven columns, no sideways scroll, and a filter panel

- `.ivInvoiceTable` is `table-layout:fixed;width:100%;min-width:0` with seven percentage widths (15/17/12/16/12/16/12) and per-cell `overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis`. Do not let it go back to a width-less table: the sheet's global `white-space:nowrap` then overflows `.ivCard` and the register scrolls sideways, which is the defect this fixed. The register card carries `.ivRegisterCard{overflow:visible}` so the Filters panel is not clipped - `.invoiceWorkspace .ivCard{overflow:auto}`.
- Columns: Invoice (number, then `{date} · {status}`, then the recurring marker), Customer, Order ID (`orderNumberOf(i)` resolves the linked order by id and falls back to `sourceOrderNumber`), Invoice amount (total, with the outstanding balance as a small line while `0 < outstanding < total`), Payment status (shared `StatusPill` + `PAYMENT_TONES`), Organisation and branch (branch over organisation), Actions (View + `InvoiceRowActions`).
- The toolbar keeps the search box and the always-visible status select and adds the sales order register's `soFiltersMore` / `soFiltersPanel` / `soClearFilters` disclosure. `INVOICE_FILTER_DEFAULTS` holds the nine field defaults; the active count, the Clear filters action and the fields all read that one object. Amount filters convert rupees to paise before comparing. An empty result distinguishes the filtered case from the first-invoice empty state.
- The invoices register's row actions are the sales order register's controls restated for this page: `.invoiceWorkspace .ivRowActions button` (36px, `0 9px`, `1px solid #d0d5dd`, 7px radius, `#344054`) and `.invoiceWorkspace .ivRowActions button.soMoreButton{margin-left:0;width:36px;height:36px;min-width:36px;min-height:36px;padding:0;color:#475467}`. They have to be more specific than `.invoiceWorkspace button` (9px 13px padding, 6px radius, `#dce3ee`, 36px min-height), which outranks the shared single-class rules and made the same component look different on this page.
- The row local is `outstandingAmount` and both the condition and the `money()` call must use it - the first attempt renamed only the condition and printed `₹NaN` because `money(due)` still read the module's `due()` date helper.

### Credit note: type, reasons, refunds, derived status

- `src/credit-note-service.js` is the single source for the credit workflow: `CREDIT_NOTE_TYPES` (Against Invoice / Without Invoice, default the first), `CREDIT_REASONS` with `canonicalReason` for the labels written earlier, `creditMappingRole` (Sales Return -> salesReturn, Tax Correction -> taxAdjustment, the pricing family -> salesAdjustment, else otherAdjustment), `isTaxOnlyReason`, `isReturnReason`, `creditedQuantity` / `creditedValue`, `CREDIT_NOTE_STATUSES` with the derived `creditNoteStatus`, `refunded`, and a `refund` command action that journals Dr A/R Cr Bank/Cash, is idempotent by token and only refunds available credit.
- `calculateCredit` branches on the type: against an invoice it keeps every existing rule and reads the tax from the invoice; without an invoice it requires customer + place of supply + one line and taxes them through the same `calculate()`. Neither branch computes tax itself, and `available()` now nets refunds off.
- `src/CreditNotes.jsx`: type selector, structured reason with the Other detail, Previously credited column, invoice-less line editor, place of supply read-only against an invoice, the shared Item Details tab row (Overview | Applications | Accounting | Activity), the Applications totals with Apply and Refund, and the derived status in the register. `src/InvoiceWorkspace.jsx` lists the invoice its related credit notes.
- Not built yet: the Sales Return document and its inventory movement. The note stores and validates `salesReturnId` and never moves stock; the create form says so. Do not let a credit note post an inventory movement - that belongs to the return document when it exists.
- Validation: `credit-note` 10/10, `credit-note-ui` 9/9, full sweep 672 tests / 661 passing / the same 11 pre-existing failures, production build passed.
- The credit note pages now render on the shared shells: the create page carries `soCreatePage ivCreatePage` with `div.soCreateHead` and the pinned `.ivFooter.soActionBar`; the register uses `.ivRegisterCard` + `table.ivInvoiceTable` + `.ivToolSearch` + the shared `StatusPill` (`CREDIT_TONES`); the detail opens on `div.ivDetailHead` with `ivDetailIdentityRow` and the shared `itemDetailTabs.ivDetailTabs` row. The register row menu is still a single View button, and the Sales Return document is still not built.

### Customer receipt detail on the shared document shell

- `src/Receipts.jsx` now opens the receipt detail through the same shell as the invoice and credit note details. `const detailOpen=!!r&&!form&&!bankView` marks `section.invoiceWorkspace.receiptWorkspace` with `ivDetailOpen`, the head is `div.ivDetailHead` (40px `button.ivDetailBack`, `aria-label="Back to customer receipts"`, `Receipt details` over `RCPT-00001 · <customer>`), and `div.ivDetailBody > section.ivDetailSheet` holds the identity row, the six figures, the tab row and one padded body.
- Keep the old heading deleted: the detail branch must not go back to `header(r.number,r.customerName,...)`, `div.ivSummary` or `div.receiptContext`. The figures are `div.ivDetailStats` (Receipt date / Payment mode / Received into / Received / Available to allocate / Allocation) and the lifecycle is the shared `StatusPill` on `RECEIPT_TONES`.
- The tab set is unchanged - Overview and Allocation always, Accounting Impact / Ledger Impact / Audit Trail behind the `Accounting details` toggle - but the row is now `nav.itemDetailTabs.ivDetailTabs` inside `div.receiptTabsRow`, with the toggle as its trailing `.receiptViewSwitch`. The row carries the divider and the nav is `border-top:0`; keep the higher-specificity `.receiptWorkspace .ivDetailSheet>.receiptTabsRow>.itemDetailTabs` selector, because `src/receipts.css` is bundled ahead of `src/invoice-detail.css`.
- `src/ReceiptImpact.jsx` uses `itemDetailTabs ivDetailTabs receiptLedgerTabs` for its three ledger views, so do not restore `div.ivTabs` there.
- `div.receiptDetailBody` replaces the `ivCard` wrapper for the tab bodies and takes `padding:18px` (12px below 720px). Print keeps `.receiptPrintHeader` and prints the sheet without a border, radius or shadow.
- Verified in headless Chrome on `RCPT-00001`: head `grid` / `8px 24px` / white with a 1px bottom rule, sheet 1122px with a 10px radius, the tab row 47px with the nav at `border-top:0` and `overflow:auto`, the active tab `rgb(36,95,217)` over `rgb(52,120,246)`, body `padding:18px`, no horizontal document overflow at 1440, and the toggle going from two tabs to five. Validation: receipt suites **20/20** (two new shell tests), full sweep **678/667/11 pre-existing failures**, production build passed.

### Purchases on the shared shells

- The Purchases destinations render inside `main` through `src/App.jsx`, not through the `createPortal` overlays `src/Navigation.jsx` used to mount: `Vendors`, `Purchase Orders`, `Purchase Bills`, `Goods Receipts`, `Debit Notes`, `Payments Made` and `Purchase Reports`. Keep `.purchasePortal` and `.vendorPortal` deleted - they only existed to float those pages above the shell, and they are why the module looked like a different product.
- Every Purchases page carries `invoiceWorkspace purchaseWorkspace`. Registers add `ivHeading` + `ivCard.ivRegisterCard` + `ivTools` (`ivToolSearch` and one `soFiltersMore` disclosure) + `ivScroll > table.ivInvoiceTable` + `ivRowActions` + `ivActions.purchaseRegisterFoot`; create pages add `soCreatePage ivCreatePage` + `div.soCreateHead` + `form.ivCreateForm` + `div.ivFooter.soActionBar`; detail pages add `ivDetailOpen` + `div.ivDetailHead` + `div.ivDetailBody > section.ivDetailSheet` + `nav.itemDetailTabs.ivDetailTabs`.
- Adding a column to a register that wears `ivInvoiceTable` means owning the widths from a selector that outranks `src/invoice-workspace.css`. That sheet declares the first seven columns as `.invoiceWorkspace .ivInvoiceTable th:nth-child(n)`, one class more specific than a page sheet bare `.receiptTable th:nth-child(n)`, so the Payment Receipts widths had been losing silently until 2026-09-21 and the page was laid out on the invoice proportions. Repeat the section class and the element - `.receiptWorkspace table.receiptTable th:nth-child(n)` - and make the set total exactly 100%. A column with no declared width collapses to zero; a column with one takes its share and every other column is scaled down to fit. The receipts register carries eight columns and the credit note register ten, each with its own declared set.
- Status is the shared `StatusPill` with a page-local tone mapper (`PURCHASE_TONES`, `DEBIT_TONES`, `VENDOR_TONES`); the row menus are `src/PurchaseRowActions.jsx`, `src/VendorRowActions.jsx` and `src/DebitNoteRowActions.jsx` on the sales order register's `.soMoreButton` / `.soActionMenu`. `src/goods-receipts.css`, `src/purchase-safety.css` and `src/purchase-workflow.css` are deleted; do not restore them.
- Service behaviour did not change: `src/purchase-service.js` posting, GST, period locking and the debit-note lifecycle, and `src/vendor-store.js`, are as they were. The vendor ledger lives inside the Vendor Details Transactions tab rather than on its own page.

## Resume checkpoint — 2026-09-17

This repository is a browser-based React/Vite accounting prototype, not a production backend. Start from the repository root and inspect `git status` before changing anything. The current branch is `main`; the last committed baseline is `897da03 Roll the register shell across the app and flatten Chart of Accounts`, already pushed to `origin/main`. The field-first Inventory Adjustment UI, the Items multi-scope/register work and the merged Item Details header panel are all committed in that baseline, so the working tree is clean. Item Details now has a full-width back header beneath Working Context, then one `section.itemDetailPanel` holding both the identity row and the Basic Data, Transactions and History tab row. The untracked `.codex-item.patch` is a local scratch artifact that `.gitignore` excludes; it is not application source and must never be committed.

Committed and pushed as `897da03 Roll the register shell across the app and flatten Chart of Accounts` (13 files, 438 insertions, 27 deletions, working tree clean): the Customers page was reworked onto the shared register + document-detail pattern. `src/Customers.jsx` now renders the register with one `Customer Details` column carrying the customer name over the customer id, the Contact person, Opening balance and Credit limit columns removed, and each row ending in one `View Customer` button beside a canonical `.customerKebab` three-dot menu (Edit Customer, Activate/Deactivate Customer, Duplicate Customer, Delete Customer); `View Customer` opens a full-width `Customer Details` screen inside the dashboard with `Basic Data`, `Transactions` (the shared `customerStatement` sub-ledger) and `History` (an additive optional `auditTrail`) tabs. Delete is refused while any invoice, payment, receipt, credit note or sales order references the customer, so a customer with history is retired by deactivation. Nothing on the page posts, stores or adjusts a balance; the validation, the `wayvida-customers` key and the `wayvida-credit-customer` statement handoff are unchanged; and Create and Edit Customer render as a full-bleed page on the Create Item shell (`form.itemCreatePage` with `div.itemDialogHead`, one `div.itemDialogBody` card of three `section.itemSection` blocks and the shared `footer.itemDialogFooter`) instead of the previous modal popup. A follow-up in the same session fixed create-page scrolling at its cause: `src/items.css` sets `overscroll-behavior:auto` on `.itemCreatePage .itemDialogBody`, because `src/ui-system.css` gives `.itemDialogBody` `overscroll-behavior:contain` and the card is `overflow:hidden`, which made the card a wheel trap - measured in headless Chrome against the running dev server, a 300px wheel over any field moved the page 0px while the same wheel over the head bar or the fixed footer moved it 300px. `src/customers.css` carries the register and detail styling and `tests/customer-register-ui.test.mjs` is the new contract test. Environment note for the next session: the default sandbox exposes `.git` read-only and blocks network access, so `git add`, `git commit`, `git pull` and `git push` fail until they are run with escalated approval, and `git pull` needs a clean tree because this history is linear (no merge commits).
Current verified runtime and architecture:

```text
index.html
└─ src/main.jsx                     application bootstrap and global providers
   └─ src/App.jsx                  shell, active-page routing, notifications, shared state
      ├─ src/Navigation.jsx        sidebar hierarchy and page labels
      ├─ src/*Workspace.jsx        accounting workspaces and register/detail/create screens
      ├─ src/*.{jsx,css}           operational modules and scoped presentation
      ├─ src/*-service.js          workflow/domain commands
      ├─ src/*-engine.js           posting, calculation and document rules
      ├─ src/*-store.js            prototype persistence adapters
      └─ localStorage/sessionStorage browser-only prototype state
```

Important boundaries:

- `App.jsx` and `Navigation.jsx` use state-driven routing; there is no React Router.
- JSX renders workflows; accounting, validation and lifecycle rules belong in pure service/engine modules.
- Accounting amounts are integer paise. Journals must balance before persistence. Posted records are corrected through reversal or adjustment.
- Working organisation/branch scope comes from `organisation-context.js`, `organisation-scope.js` and `OrganisationBranchScope.jsx`; do not create private scope readers.
- The main accounting aggregate is handled through `account-store.js`; feature-specific prototype records also use documented browser-storage keys. Browser storage is not secure, transactional or shared.
- Reports consume posted journals. Budgets are a planning layer and never post. Orders do not post. Inventory Adjustments post only after reaching `Adjusted`.
- UI roles and the Business owner / Head of Accountant terminology switch are simulations, never authenticated authorization.

Latest Inventory Adjustment contract:

- Navigation is `Inventory > Inventory Adjustments`.
- The register supports search, one Filters disclosure, pagination, detail opening and status-aware row actions.
- Create/Edit is field-first with no Step labels or Adjustment details / Items heading copy.
- Field order and labels: Mode of adjustment; Quantity Adjustment / Value Adjustment; Reference Number; Date; Adjustment location; Account; Reason; Description.
- Quantity grid labels: Item Details; Location; Quantity Available; New Quantity on hand; Quantity Adjusted. The editable and derived cells swap when Entry mode changes so the column meaning remains truthful.
- A missing stock-item dependency shows one Create Item recovery action, disables Add Item and final posting/submission, and leaves Save as Draft available.
- Draft/Cancelled records do not move stock or post. Adjusted records update the derived inventory position and create one balanced journal. Posted corrections use reversal.

Validation at this checkpoint: `node --test --test-isolation=none tests/inventory-adjustments.test.mjs` passed 25/25; `node scripts/validate-knowledge-docs.mjs` passed; the Vite production build passed with 6,959 modules and the existing bundle-size advisory; `scripts/prepare-sites-build.mjs` refreshed Sites artifacts; the running page at port 4001 was inspected without submitting a transaction. Customers checkpoint in the same session: `node --test "tests/**/*.test.mjs"` reported 495 tests with 484 passing and the same 11 pre-existing failures (account, journal, settings, period-lock, operational-module and date-picker suites whose pinned sources this change never touched, confirmed by `git status --short` listing only `src/Customers.jsx`, `src/customers.css` and the new `tests/customer-register-ui.test.mjs`), the new customers contract test is 8/8, `node scripts/validate-knowledge-docs.mjs` passed, and the Vite production build passed with 6,959 modules and the existing bundle-size advisory, emitting a new `dist/client/assets/index-B-S5DWlo.css`. Follow-up in the same session: the Create/Edit Customer popup was converted to a full page on the Create Item shell; `tests/customer-register-ui.test.mjs` is 8/8, `tests/item-create-page-ui.test.mjs` is 9/9, the full sweep is still 495 tests with 484 passing and the same 11 pre-existing failures, the dev server on port 4001 transforms `/src/Customers.jsx` without error, and the Vite build passed again with 6,959 modules (`dist/client/assets/index-CUSwfIne.js`). Scrolling fix validation: `tests/item-create-page-ui.test.mjs` is 10/10 with the new wheel-chaining contract, the full sweep is 496 tests with 485 passing and the same 11 pre-existing failures, the headless Chrome sweep over the dev server now moves the create page from every pointer position (300px moved at y=80, 160, 240, 320, 400, 480 and 560), and the Vite build passes again with 6,959 modules (`dist/client/assets/index-Buknbl28.css`).

Newest feature (2026-09-17): Items supports multi-organisation and multi-branch availability with the shared `OrganisationBranchScope multiple` control. Stored items add `organizationIds`, `branchIds`, `active` and `auditTrail` while preserving the legacy primary `organizationId` and `warehouseId`. The register shows Item name + SKU + HSN/SAC and uses View Details plus a three-dot Edit Item / Enable or Disable Item / Duplicate Item / Delete menu. The details page shows the complete stored master, linked journals/invoices/purchases/inventory adjustments, and local audit history. Opening-stock accounting is unchanged. Tests: the focused Items set is 22 tests after this feature's new suite; final totals are recorded in QA.

Newest change (2026-09-17): the Item Details identity card and tab row were merged into one header panel at the user's request, because the two read as detached blocks - a white identity card with a separate segmented pill floating under it. src/Items.jsx now wraps both in section.itemDetailPanel. src/items.css gives the panel the single card edge (background:#fff;border:1px solid #e6ebf2;border-radius:10px) and strips the border, radius and background off .itemDetailIdentity; the panel must never carry overflow:hidden or it would clip the More actions menu that opens from the identity row. The tab bar is now the panel footer rather than a pill: display:flex;align-items:center;flex:0 0 auto;gap:0;height:46px;padding:0 4px;border-top:1px solid #e6ebf2;background:transparent;overflow:hidden, tabs stay content-sized (flex:0 0 auto;width:auto) at 0 12px padding with no vertical separators, and the active tab is blue text over a 2px #3478f6 bottom border. The 4px bar padding plus the tab's 12px padding lines the first label up with the 16px card gutter, dropping to 0 below 700px to follow that breakpoint's 12px gutter; below 520px the tabs still fall back to equal flex:1 1 0 cells at 0 8px/12px. width:auto remains load-bearing - src/styles.css declares a bare nav button{width:100%}, and with flex-basis:auto that width would make every tab as wide as the whole bar and let overflow:hidden clip Transactions and History, which is the regression the user reported earlier. The tab-row test in tests/items-master-workspace-ui.test.mjs is now the item identity card and tab row are one header panel and the tabs never scroll, and it asserts the panel wrapper, the panel rule, the absence of overflow:hidden on the panel and the new tab geometry. No storage, posting, validation or routing contract changed. Previous 2026-09-17 change: the Item Details tab strip was tightened at the user's request. `.itemDetailTabs` had given each of the three tabs an equal `flex:1 1 0` third of the full content width, which on a wide screen pushed the Basic Data, Transactions and History labels hundreds of pixels apart. The tabs are now content-sized (`flex:0 0 auto`, `width:auto`, `0 10px` padding) and the bar carries `align-self:flex-start` so it shrinks to them, while keeping `gap:0;padding:0;overflow:hidden`, the 1px separators, the `#3478f6` active bottom border and the explicit `height:46px` that stops an ancestor stretching the bar into a tall empty box. A regression followed the first release and is fixed: `src/styles.css` declares a bare `nav button{width:100%}`, and because `flex:0 0 auto` resolves `flex-basis` to `auto` that width became the flex base size of every tab, so each tab claimed the whole strip and `overflow:hidden` clipped Transactions and History off the right edge - exactly what the user reported. `width:auto` on `.itemDetailTabs button` is what prevents it and must not be removed; `flex:1 1 0` had only been masking the same rule behind `flex-basis:0`. The narrow-viewport fallback (`.itemDetailTabs{align-self:stretch}` with `flex:1 1 0`, `0 8px` padding and a 12px label) now lives in its own `@media(max-width:520px)` block declared after the base rules, because its earlier placement inside the shared 520px block sat above them and lost at equal specificity, so it never applied at all. `tests/items-master-workspace-ui.test.mjs` was updated to pin the new geometry; its tab-row test was later renamed to `the item identity card and tab row are one header panel and the tabs never scroll` when the panel merge landed. No storage, posting, validation or routing contract changed. Previous 2026-09-17 change: the Item Details page had a spacing pass - one 14px rhythm between the identity card, the tab strip and the section cards, 16px of padding inside each card, a 48px identity tile, 36px Edit Item and More-actions controls, and no duplicated body bottom margin, so the page ends on the existing 32px bottom padding; `src/Items.jsx` adds the saved SKU to the identity meta line. Reported by the user as a screenshot showing a large empty grey block above the tabs; that exact geometry is not reproducible from this working tree's cascade, so treat a stale page or a cached stylesheet as the likely cause and measure the live page before changing anything else.

Newest change (2026-09-17): New / Edit Inventory Adjustment is now field-first. The Step 1 / Step 2 / Step 3 labels, Adjustment details and Items headings, and their explanatory copy are removed. The form labels and order are Mode of adjustment (Quantity Adjustment / Value Adjustment), Reference Number, Date, Adjustment location, Account, Reason and Description. The item grid reads Item Details, Location, Quantity Available, New Quantity on hand and Quantity Adjusted, with the input/derived cells swapping correctly for Set New Quantity. The existing no-item recovery, accounting disclosure and sticky footer remain. No stored field or accounting behavior changed.

Previous checkpoint, superseded in part by the field-first layout above (2026-09-17): New / Edit Inventory Adjustment introduced `div.iaCreateHead`, a full-width document header flush beneath Working Context. The header geometry, responsive gutter and scroll reset remain current; the former `iaItemsGuide`, Items heading and step copy were subsequently removed.

Newest change (2026-09-16): Create Item renders Inventory Asset Account first and Cost of Goods Sold Account second in the same responsive Track inventory row. Advanced settings contains the single Opening stock disclosure, Tax settings and the minimal Item image attachment row, in that order. Opening-stock calculations, validation and posting are unchanged. Item image remains directly below its label and retains the native input, MIME validation, 2 MB limit, FileReader, preview and removal contracts. Create Item uses one scoped document-level vertical scroll surface: `html`, `body` and `#root` become auto-height/scrollable while `.app` and `main` release the shell height/overflow locks, so wheel, trackpad, touch and scrollbar input act on the page instead of competing with a nested main scroller. Fixed actions retain bottom clearance.

Newest change (2026-09-16): Create Item no longer relies on body scrolling. For this page `html`, `body` and `.app` are viewport-bounded, while `.app > main` is the one vertical scroll port (`height:calc(100vh - 58px);overflow-y:auto;scroll-behavior:smooth;overscroll-behavior-y:contain;scrollbar-gutter:auto`), so wheel, trackpad, touch, keyboard and scrollbar dragging act on the same surface. The fixed Cancel/Create bar and 88px form clearance remain. Description was removed from the UI only; keep `description` in `blankItem` and saved records so legacy data survives edits. Advanced settings now renders the unchanged image upload logic as a standard attachment card with `IconPhotoPlus`, a visually hidden native file input, Choose file treatment, the existing JPG/PNG/WebP · Maximum 2 MB guidance, and a responsive ready-image preview/removal row.

Newest change (2026-09-16): Create Item scrolls as one smooth document with vertical overscroll containment, while its Cancel / Create Item action card is truly viewport-fixed rather than bottom-sticky (a bottom-sticky element is not visible until its normal document position enters the viewport). The bar uses the 270px desktop sidebar plus the page gutter for its left edge, drops to 12px side offsets below 760px and becomes edge-to-edge below 600px; `.itemCreatePage` carries 88px bottom clearance so no field is obscured. Item Type remains a native Goods / Service radio group but is now two balanced selectable cards with an 18px circular indicator, blue selected border/tint, hover and focus states. The more-specific radio rule must stay beneath the shared `.itemFormGrid input:not([type=file])` rule so the radio never becomes a full-width rectangular text control.

Newest change (2026-09-16): Create Item kept its established full-bleed Wayvida layout but now follows the requested field hierarchy. Item Type is a Goods-default native Goods / Service radio group, and the one persisted `hsnSac` value is labelled HSN Code or SAC Code dynamically. The existing optional `description` field is visible once; SKU, HSN/SAC and Unit remain one responsive row. Sell, Purchase and Track inventory remain value-preserving switches. Exactly one new-item Opening stock disclosure sits below the tracked-inventory accounts; Advanced settings contains only the existing 2 MB JPG/PNG/WebP upload. Tax settings uses Taxable / Non-taxable and keeps its four GST controls and helper only while taxable. Compact section labels use `div.itemSectionTitle`, not `header`. Storage, validation, tax calculation and opening-journal behavior are unchanged; browser verification covered Goods/Service, taxable/non-taxable and expanded Opening stock states.
**Audience:** ChatGPT, Codex, Claude, Gemini, Google AI Studio, Lovable, Antigravity, and human engineers.

This is the starting point for continuing the project in another AI account or tool. Transfer the complete repository when possible. If that is impossible, transfer the entire `docs/` folder together with `package.json`, `src/`, `tests/`, `public/`, `scripts/`, `worker/`, `.openai/`, `vite.config.mjs`, and `AGENTS.md`.

## 1. Current project state

- Product: Wayvida Books accounting ERP browser prototype.
- Repository location is environment-specific. Work from the root containing `package.json`, `AGENTS.md`, `src/`, `tests/` and `docs/`; never hard-code a previous machine path.
- Stack: React 19.2, JavaScript ES modules, Vite 6.4, Tabler icons, and Recharts.
- Persistence: browser `localStorage` and `sessionStorage`; there is no production database or authenticated backend.
- Navigation: state-driven navigation in `src/App.jsx` and `src/Navigation.jsx`; React Router is not used.
- Accounting engine: operational documents use domain services to create balanced journals. Amounts in accounting engines are integer paise.
- Current build: production build verified 2026-09-17 with 6,959 modules transformed. Vite reports the existing non-blocking large-chunk advisory.

## 2. Latest completed feature

Newest change (2026-09-16): the Create Item head bar is flush under the working-context Organisation / Branch bar. The bar itself was already on the journal geometry, but `main` still carried the shell box from `src/ui-system.css` (`.app main{padding:22px clamp(16px,2.2vw,32px) 40px;max-width:1680px}`), so a 22px gap sat above the bar and a 32px inset beside it; `src/items.css` now removes both with `.app main:has(>.itemsPage:has(.itemCreatePage)),body:has(.itemsPage .itemCreatePage) .app main{max-width:none!important;padding:0!important}`. The rule is written in two shapes and both carry `!important` - the direct-child `:has()` shape alone, without `!important`, was not reaching the bar in the running app even though the sibling `.itemCreatePage .itemDialogHead` rule did - and the head gutter is now `.orgContextBar`'s own `padding:8px 24px`, narrowing to 12px with the org bar at 760px, so the back button lines up with the building icon. Diagnosed by measuring the operator's screenshot (1902x246 at 1.25 dpr: 270px sidebar = 337 image px, org bar ends y=126, head bar top border y=155, bar spans x=378..1860) rather than by a browser pass, which this environment refuses.

Newest change (2026-09-16): the Create Item form lost its five section headings and the dark blue line that sat above them. Each heading was a bare `<header>` element, and `src/styles.css` styles every `header` as the application bar (`.app header{border-top:3px solid #1f5fae;height:59px;padding:8px 18px;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 1px 0 #e7ebf1}`); because the shell imports `./App.jsx` - and `src/items.css` through it - ahead of `./styles.css`, the earlier `.itemSection>header` reset lost at equal specificity and every heading was painted as a white 59px block with a 3px dark blue top border. `src/Items.jsx` is now `function Section({children}){return <section className='itemSection'>{children}</section>}` and no call site passes a `title`, so the page renders no `<header>` and no `<h3>` at all while the five `.itemSection` containers and their hairline dividers stay, and the `.itemSection>header` rules were deleted from `src/items.css`. The head bar now matches the Create Journal Entry compact bar: `.itemCreatePage .itemDialogHead` is `display:grid;grid-template-columns:42px minmax(0,1fr);align-items:center;gap:14px;min-height:56px;padding:8px clamp(16px,2.2vw,24px);border:1px solid var(--ui-border);border-left:0;border-right:0;border-radius:0;box-shadow:none`, the same shape as `.je-create > header.je-header-compact` on this page's own gutter. Tests: `tests/item-create-page-ui.test.mjs` 7/7 (`item-tax-ui` 4/4, `ui-quality` 5/5); the full sweep is 78 files with 63 passing and the same 15 pre-existing failures.

Newest change (2026-09-16): the Create Item page was put on Create Journal Entry's shell, without touching any other module. `src/items.css` gives the item form the same single-document scroll the journal page uses - `html:has(.itemsPage .itemCreatePage)`, `body:has(.itemsPage .itemCreatePage)`, `.app:has(>main>.itemsPage:has(.itemCreatePage))` and `main` all stop being the scroll port - so the full-bleed head bar that carries `Create Item` keeps the application header's width instead of losing a gutter to `scrollbar-gutter:stable` on `.app>main`, and the Cancel / Create Item row became a static white card under the form instead of a sticky translucent bar. Both page gutters are now `clamp(16px,2.2vw,24px)` so the head bar and its body line up with the `padding:8px 24px` Organisation and Branch bar. `src/Items.jsx` passes no `description` to any `Section`, so the five section cards render only their title and the `Basic information` / `Purchase information` / `Inventory information` / `Tax information` sentences are gone; the `.itemSection>header` reset stays, because `src/styles.css` styles a bare `header` element as the 58px fixed application bar. Tests: `tests/item-create-page-ui.test.mjs` 7/7 (its new contract: the page scrolls as one document, the action bar is static and no section carries a description), `tests/item-tax-ui.test.mjs` 4/4, `tests/item-master.test.mjs` 4/4; the full sweep is 78 files with 63 passing and the same 15 pre-existing failures.

Newest change (2026-09-16): the Inventory Adjustments register was brought into line with the other accounting registers. `src/InventoryAdjustments.jsx` now opens with a plain `div.iaHeading` title row instead of the `.opsHead` card, and its toolbar is `div.iaRegisterBar` - the search box beside one `details.iaFilters` disclosure that holds every dropdown (Adjustment type, Status, Organisation, Branch / location, From date, To date) and a `Clear filters` row, with the design-system `IconFilter` icon on the summary, one count of active filters and no record count - while the ten register headings are plain labels: the sort buttons, `aria-sort`, `SORTERS`, the `sort` state and `toggleSort` are gone. The page also carries no acting-as selector any more: `RoleField`, `ADJUSTMENT_ROLES` and the `.iaRole` rules are deleted and the acting role comes from the header account menu's View as switch through `useTerminology()` - `Business owner` acts as Admin, `Head of Accountant` as Accountant. Module behaviour is unchanged: only an `Adjusted` document moves inventory and posts one balanced entry. `tests/inventory-adjustments.test.mjs` 21/21.

Newest change (2026-09-16): every table or list that can be empty now renders one shared empty state instead of a bare sentence, centred inside the table's own content area. `src/EmptyState.jsx` is the single component (`EmptyState({variant,title,description,action,actionLabel,onAction,className})` plus the named `EmptyIllustration`, with the variants `budget`, `lock`, `request`, `journal`, `adjustment` and `default` sharing one plate, one 2.4 stroke weight and one grey) and `src/empty-state.css` is its only styling: a centred flex column bounded at `min-height:250px` with `32px 20px` padding, a 96px illustration (82px below 640px), a 15px title, a 13px description and an optional primary action that reuses the primary button. It is wired as a `tr.emptyStateRow > td.emptyStateCell` row inside the table body in the Budgets register, the Period Lock lock register and its mobile card list, the Journal Entries accountant register and the Inventory Adjustments register, and the unlock request register uses it without an action. The copy is `No budgets found` / `No locks created yet` / `No unlock requests yet` / `No journals found` / `No adjustments found`, each with its own sentence and, except for unlock requests, its own create action. The old inline markup was replaced rather than duplicated - the budget `budgetEmpty` register cell, the Period Lock mode-aware `emptyText` line and drawer sentence, and the legacy journal `je-empty` block, whose removal also dropped an unreachable duplicate `return` statement in `src/JournalEntriesPro.jsx`. `tests/empty-state-ui.test.mjs` is new and now passes 6/6 after the animation contract was added, `tests/budget-register-ui.test.mjs` is green again at 7/7 after being made line-ending independent (this repository checks out with `core.autocrlf=true`, so a bare LF never matches a source built with CRLF), and a full sweep gives 62 files passing and 15 failing against 60 and 16 before, with every remaining failure pre-existing and in another module. On the user's follow-up the illustrations were animated, again inside `src/empty-state.css` alone: each shape draws itself in over roughly a second (`.emptyArt` fade-and-rise, `.emptyArtPlate` 0.9 to 1 scale, the three `.emptyBar` budget bars staggered, the `.emptyDraw` / `.emptyDrawLate` strokes through `stroke-dashoffset`, an `.emptyDot` pulse) and then rests on a slow 7s `emptyBreathe`; the base drawing is complete when no animation runs and `@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)` removes all of it, while `src/EmptyState.jsx` only gained those hook classes, so no markup contract, copy, layout or accounting behaviour changed.

Newest change (2026-09-16): the Create Item / Edit Item form became more compact and now states its GST treatment in Indian terms, without any other module changing. The page header lost its supporting sentence and reads `Create Item` (or `Edit Item`) alone; the Category dropdown is gone from the form - `category` is no longer collected and `blankItem` still stores its `General` default for stored items - and SKU, HSN/SAC and Unit share one three-across `.itemFormGrid3` row that stacks below 700px. Tax information keeps the existing `.itemSection` card and adds no new one: `Taxable` is a compact Yes / No control over `taxPreference`, and while it is Yes the section shows `Intra-State Tax Rate`, `Inter-State Tax Rate`, the existing cess and `Price includes tax?` fields, the sentence `Intra-state transactions use CGST + SGST. Inter-state transactions use IGST.` and a read-only `dl.itemTaxPreview` that derives the split - the intra-state rate is the total intra-state GST and is halved into CGST and SGST, the inter-state rate is IGST - through the pure `gstSplit(rate)` helper in `src/item-master.js`. While it is No the two rate fields are not rendered, and a `Tax treatment` select stores `Non-taxable`, `Exempt`, `Zero Rated` or `Non-GST`. Rate options come from the shared `ITEM_TAX_RATES` master (`0`, `5`, `12`, `18`, `28`), and a taxable item still stores the intra-state rate in `taxRate`, which is the field `src/SalesDocumentFields.jsx` and `src/Purchases.jsx` already read to build CGST + SGST or IGST on a line - so no tax calculation downstream changed. `tests/item-tax-ui.test.mjs` 4/4, `tests/item-master.test.mjs` 4/4.

Newest change (2026-09-15): HTTP VM deploys threw `crypto.randomUUID is not a function` during demo bootstrap because `randomUUID` is secure-context-only. `src/crypto-uuid.js` now installs a UUID v4 fallback (via `getRandomValues`) when the method is missing; `index.html` runs the same install before the app module, and `src/main.jsx` / `src/audit-log.js` import the helper first. Existing `crypto.randomUUID()` call sites are unchanged. `tests/crypto-uuid.test.mjs` pins the fallback and the HTML boot order. No storage keys, routing, or accounting behaviour changed.

Newest change (2026-09-15): deployed UI was blank because Vite only allowed `terminal.local` and nginx could serve `index.html` as a JavaScript module. `vite.config.mjs` now uses `allowedHosts: true` on the 4001 dev and preview servers, and `docker/nginx.conf` 404s missing `/assets/` files, sets JS MIME types, and disables HTML caching. Instrument Sans is linked from `index.html` rather than a blocking CSS `@import`. `src/main.jsx` surfaces a start error instead of leaving `#root` empty. `tests/vite-port.test.mjs` and `tests/docker-vm.test.mjs` pin the host and nginx contracts. No storage, routing, or accounting behaviour changed.

Newest change (2026-09-15): the Docker VM host port is 4002. `docker-compose.yml` maps `${WAYVIDA_PORT:-4002}:80`, so `docker compose up --build -d` is available at `http://<host>:4002/`. Local Vite stays on 4001. `tests/docker-vm.test.mjs` pins the Docker default. No image, nginx, Sites, storage, or accounting behaviour changed.

Newest change (2026-09-15): local Vite now defaults to port 4001 as well. `vite.config.mjs` sets `server.port` and `preview.port` to 4001 with `strictPort`, and `package.json` `dev` / `preview` scripts pass `--port 4001 --strictPort`, so `npm run dev` opens `http://localhost:4001/` instead of 5173. Docker Compose already used 4001. `tests/vite-port.test.mjs` pins the local default. No routing, storage, or accounting behaviour changed.

Newest change (2026-09-15): the Docker VM host port is 4001. `docker-compose.yml` maps `${WAYVIDA_PORT:-4001}:80`, so `docker compose up --build -d` is available at `http://<host>:4001/` without an env override. `tests/docker-vm.test.mjs` pins the new default. No image, nginx, Sites, storage, or accounting behaviour changed.

Newest change (2026-09-15): the prototype can run on a VM through Docker Compose. `Dockerfile` is a two-stage image (Node 22 Alpine build, nginx 1.27 Alpine serve of `dist/client`), `docker-compose.yml` publishes host port 4001 (`WAYVIDA_PORT` overrides it), and `docker/nginx.conf` keeps the Sites SPA `try_files` fallback while `/api/` stays 404. `npm run docker:up` / `docker:down` wrap compose. `react-is` was added so `recharts` can complete `vite build` inside the image. This is static prototype hosting only: browser `localStorage` stays in the client, and the container is not a production accounting backend. Sites files are unchanged. `tests/docker-vm.test.mjs` pins the image, compose port, nginx fallback and ignore list.

Newest change (2026-09-15): unused local junk was removed from the workspace. The scan found every `src/` module still referenced, so no product source, test, or required knowledge document was deleted. Removed: `tmp/` scratch appliers, browser profiles (`.edge-cdp-profile/`, `.tmp-edge-profile/`), `.pnpm-store/`, empty `qa-evidence/`, generated `dist/`, unused page-screenshot folder and zip, leftover QA PNGs, the accidental `const a = \`x\`;` file, historical `design-qa.md`, unused `vite.sandbox.config.mjs`, and leftover `pnpm-lock.yaml` / `pnpm-workspace.yaml` (this project installs with npm and `package-lock.json`). `.gitignore` now keeps those artifacts out. No routing, storage, accounting, or UI behaviour changed.

Newest change (2026-09-15): the Budget section is now a single `Budgets` destination inside Accounting. On the user instruction Budget Reports, Budget Settings and the Budget sub-group left the Accounting section, so `src/Navigation.jsx` declares `{label:'Accounting',icon:IconBook,children:[leaf('Chart of Accounts'),leaf('Journal Entries'),leaf('Budgets'),leaf('Period Lock')]}` and drops the now-unused `IconChartBar` import. `src/BudgetWorkspace.jsx` deleted the `BudgetReports` and `BudgetSettings` components, their two `page` routes and the `IconFilter` / `BUDGET_PERMISSIONS` / `emptyBudgetState` imports they alone used, and reworded the plan Overview alert hint from `Alerts follow the thresholds in Budget Settings.` to `Alerts follow the saved alert thresholds.`. `src/budget-workspace.css` deleted the 24 rules those screens owned and trimmed the two media queries that named them, and `src/App.jsx` now routes the page with `a==='Budgets'` alone because the two extra names can never be active. The register, the create wizard, the plan detail tabs, the store, the storage keys and every budget calculation are untouched. `tests/navigation-structure.test.mjs` (2/2) and `tests/budget-navigation-ui.test.mjs` (3/3) were re-pointed, `tests/budget-register-ui.test.mjs` stays 7/7, `tmp/budget-nav-ssr.mjs` proves the rendered sidebar has no Budget group, and the sweep stays `FILES=72 green=58 red=10 tests=414 passed=403 failed=11` with the same ten pre-existing failures and the same four esbuild-blocked suites. Not verified here and not claimed: no painted browser pass and no production build (`npm` and `npx` are absent and esbuild cannot spawn, so `vite build` fails with `spawn EPERM`).

Newest change (2026-09-15): the Budget group moved back under Accounting. On the user instruction the Budget section no longer has its own top-level sidebar slot between Accounting and Inventory and is now nested inside the Accounting section, between Journal Entries and Period Lock. `src/Navigation.jsx` declares `{label:'Accounting',icon:IconBook,children:[leaf('Chart of Accounts'),leaf('Journal Entries'),{label:'Budget',icon:IconChartBar,children:[leaf('Budgets'),leaf('Budget Reports'),leaf('Budget Settings')]},leaf('Period Lock')]}` and the Inventory section is back to a single `Items` child, so the three budget leaves keep their names and the group's ids become `Accounting/Budget` and `nav-Accounting-Budget`. No `src/App.jsx` change was needed, because it routes budgets by page name and hides the welcome block for those pages, and the shared `nav.budgetWorkspaceTabs` row, the storage keys and every budget calculation are untouched. `tests/navigation-structure.test.mjs` (2/2) and `tests/budget-navigation-ui.test.mjs` (3/3) were re-pointed to the nested placement and the Journal Entries < Budget < Period Lock order, `tmp/budget-nav-ssr.mjs` proves the rendered sidebar, and the sweep stays `FILES=72 green=58 red=10 tests=414 passed=403 failed=11` with the same ten pre-existing failures and the same four esbuild-blocked suites. Newest change (2026-09-15): the Create Budget wizard now states its scope once, reports progress on the stepper connectors and groups its footer buttons. On the user instruction the details step no longer prints `Planning for <organisation> · <branch>.`, the progress connection line shows completed, current and pending separately, and the primary and secondary buttons sit together on the right. `src/BudgetWorkspace.jsx` deleted the `<p className="budgetScopeHint">` line so the step states the plan width once, through the `span.budgetScopeSummary` chip in its `budgetSelectHead`; `scopePickerState()` still builds the `scope` value and `save({...form,scope})` still stores it, so no stored plan or reopened scope changes. `src/budget-workspace.css` deleted the two dead `.budgetScopeHint` rules, gave the 2px `li:not(:last-child)::after` connector one state per step (`var(--budget-blue)` behind `li.complete`, a half `linear-gradient(90deg,var(--budget-blue) 0,var(--budget-blue) 50%,#e1e6ed 50%,#e1e6ed 100%)` behind `li.active`, `#e8edf3` behind `li.upcoming`) and removed the `.budgetWizardActions button:first-child:not(.primary){margin-right:auto}` split so `Cancel` / `Back` sit immediately left of the one primary action in the static right-aligned footer. `tests/budget-create-flow-ui.test.mjs` now asserts the hint is gone, the two new connector tokens and the removed split, and `tmp/budget-create-ssr.mjs` asserts the hint is gone plus the rendered stepper states. Newest change (2026-09-15): Create Budget and the budget detail screen were refined in place, keeping the three-step workflow and the Wayvida visual language. `src/budget-store.js` gained `'Half-Yearly'` in `BUDGET_PERIODS`, `startDate` / `endDate` on `createBudget`, `logBudgetActivity`, `parseBudgetCSV(text,accounts)` and `varianceDirection(type,variance)`, and `computeVarianceRows` now returns `{account,budget,actual,variance,percent,achievement,direction}` with `variance` as `actual - planned` and `achievement` as the separate Actual-over-Budget figure. `src/budget-statements.js` renamed its total labels to `Total Revenue`, `Total Expenses`, `Net Profit / Loss`, `Profit Margin`, `Total Assets`, `Total Liabilities`, `Total Equity` and `Total Liabilities & Equity`, added `budgetBalanceCheck(accounts,allocations,period)`, and added a `kind:'check'` `Balance Difference` row to the balance sheet. `src/BudgetWorkspace.jsx` now groups the allocation grid under `BUDGET_ALLOCATION_BANDS` with a sticky account column and foldable type rows, adds Import Budget / Clear All and a Growth % input, adds a `section.budgetBalanceCheck`, puts step one's Start date, End date and Description on the field grid with a `budgetScopeSummary` chip and step two's account-type filter, `Clear all` and `budgetSelectedCount` above a foldable `AccountSelectGrid`, replaces the detail tab list with the flat `BUDGET_DETAIL_TABS` `[key,label]` pairs and `BUDGET_DETAIL_DEFAULT='Overview'`, moves the KPI cards into `BudgetOverview`, rewrites Budget vs Actual with Variance % and Achievement %, and adds `BudgetAccountsTable` and `BudgetDetailsPanel` while deleting `AccountTree` and `ForecastPanel`. Newest change (2026-09-15): Create Budget -> Budget Allocation now closes with a Budget totals section. `section.budgetTotals` inside `section.budgetAllocation` holds `div.budgetTotalsGrid`, a table sharing the allocation grid period columns plus one row total, over the statement lines Total for Revenue, Total for Expense, Total for Profit and Loss, Profit margin, Total for Assets, Total for Liabilities, Total for Equities, Total for Liabilities & Equities and Budget Mismatch Adjustment. Every figure is computed in `BudgetWizard` from `form.allocations` (`planned`, `periodSums`, `sumValues`, `marginOf`, `totalsRows`), so nothing new is stored: no storage key, engine function, allocation, revision, variance, forecast, alert or settings behaviour changed, and `src/budget-store.js` is untouched. The four `budgetReviewTotals` chips were removed.
